Do not edit by hand.\r\n// Regenerate with: node scripts/build-guided-drills-local-seed.mjs\r\n// Bundled so guided-drill launches resolve their pool client-side even when the\r\n// live DB row is missing or lacks a bank_filter. See src/lib/guidedDrillsLocal.ts.\r\n\r\nexport const GUIDED_DRILLS_LOCAL_SEED = [\r\n  {\r\n    "slug": "w4-01-reconcile-the-pivot",\r\n    "title": "Reconcile the Pivot",\r\n    "category": "Pacing",\r\n    "estimated_minutes": 40,\r\n    "question_count": 5,\r\n    "difficulty_min": 2,\r\n    "difficulty_max": 4,\r\n    "level": "intermediate",\r\n    "question_types": [\r\n      "Most Strongly Supported",\r\n      "Must Be True"\r\n    ],\r\n    "question_features": [\r\n      "Important Pivot"\r\n    ],\r\n    "reasoning_types": [\r\n      "Comparison"\r\n    ],\r\n    "start_button_label": "Start",\r\n    "instruction_card": {\r\n      "left_md": "Within the world of Inference (**Most Supported / Must Be True**), we are trying to combine 2 or more ideas together in order to derive some synthesis of ideas.\\n\\nWe often can\'t predict what the correct answer will say, but we often can, if we pay attention to certain \\"clue words\\":\\n\\n- conditional indicators (if, only if, unless, requires, etc.)\\n- causal relationships (because of this, due to this, this allows, this makes possible, as a result, this leads to, etc.)\\n- quantifiers (most, some, all, no)\\n- other mathematical ideas (percents, fractions, numbers, ratios)\\n- pivots (but, yet, however)\\n\\nThe pattern we call _Reconcile the Pivot_ is when the last claim begins with a Pivot word. That provides a strong clue that we\'re supposed to reconcile that final fact with what came before it.",\r\n      "right_md": "# Try these 5 problems in 8 mins\\n\\n1.5x: 12 mins / 2x: 16 mins\\n\\n- When you hit the pivot word (but / yet / however), think about how that new claim creates some tension with what came before or how it\'s relevant to the preceding ideas.\\n\\n- Try to mentally articulate what the net effect is of combining that pivot idea with what came before. Expect the answer to deal with whatever it is you\'re thinking in that moment.\\n\\n- Since these are Most Supported questions, remember that _Strong Language_ in the answer choices is a big red flag. We want the most provable answer, so weaker claims (with a lower burden of proof) are more attractive.\\n\\n---"\r\n    },\r\n'}]

→\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t18 text TIME\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t19 text 40 min\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t20 text QUESTIONS\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t21 text 5\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t22 text SCROLL DOWN FOR NOTES ↓\n\t\t\t\t\t\t23 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t24 container section-1\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t25 text Briefing\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t26 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t27 text Within the world of Inference ( Most Supported / Must Be True ), we are trying to combine 2 or more ideas together in order to derive some synthesis of ideas.\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t28 text We often can\'t predict what the correct answer will say, but we often can, if we pay attention to certain "clue words":\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t29 content list\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t30 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t31 AXListMarker • \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t32 text conditional indicators (if, only if, unless, requires, etc.)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t33 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t34 AXListMarker • \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t35 text causal relationships (because of this, due to this, this allows, this makes possible, as a result, this leads to, etc.)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t36 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t37 AXListMarker • \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t38 text quantifiers (most, some, all, no)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t39 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t40 AXListMarker • \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t41 text other mathematical ideas (percents, fractions, numbers, ratios)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t42 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t43 AXListMarker • \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t44 text pivots (but, yet, however)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t45 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t46 text The pattern we call  Reconcile the Pivot  is when the last claim begins with a Pivot word. That provides a strong clue that we\'re supposed to reconcile that final fact with what came before it.\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t47 container section-2\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t48 text How to Read This Set\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t49 text Try these 5 problems in 8 mins\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t50 text 1.5x: 12 mins / 2x: 16 mins\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t51 content list\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t52 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t53 AXListMarker • \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t54 text When you hit the pivot word (but / yet / however), think about how that new claim creates some tension with what came before or how it\'s relevant to the preceding ideas.\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t55 content list\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t56 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t57 AXListMarker • \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t58 text Try to mentally articulate what the net effect is of combining that pivot idea with what came before. Expect the answer to deal with whatever it is you\'re thinking in that moment.\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t59 content list\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t60 container\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t61 AXListMarker • \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t62 text Since these are Most Supported questions, remember that  Strong Language  in the answer choices is a big red flag. (requestedStandaloneRunId ? null : await loadGuidedDrillSession(\r\n          user.id, guidedSessionKey, guidedLegacySessionKey,\r\n        ));\r\n        guidedLegacyResumeRef.current = !ownedRun && stored?.status === \'in_progress\' && stored.attempts.length > 0;\r\n        const canResume = ownedRun\r\n          ? stored?.status === \'in_progress\' && stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&\r\n            stored.question_queue.every((qid) => !!questionBank.getQuestion(qid))\r\n          : isResumableGuidedSession(stored, questionQueue);\r\n        if (canResume && stored) {\r\n          questionQueue = stored.question_queue;\r\n          initialIndex = guidedResumeIndex(\r\n            questionQueue,\r\n            stored.current_index,\r\n            stored.attempts.map((a) => a.qid),\r\n          );\r\n          for (const attempt of stored.attempts) {\r\n            restoredAttempts.set(attempt.qid, {\r\n              selectedAnswer: attempt.selected_answer,\r\n              correct: attempt.correct,\r\n              timeMs: attempt.time_ms,\r\n              timestamp: Date.now(),\r\n              confidence: undefined,\r\n              reviewDone: false,\r\n              // The record, not just the answer. A resumed run used to come\r\n              // back flattened: no changes of mind, no marks for review.\r\n              switchCount: attempt.switch_count,\r\n              brMarked: attempt.flagged,\r\n              answerRevealed: attempt.answer_revealed ?? false,\r\n              answerTrail: attempt.answer_trail?.map((e) => ({\r\n                answer: e.answer,\r\n                atMs: e.at_ms,\r\n              })),\r\n            });\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (stored?.status === \'in_progress\' && stored.attempts.length > 0) {\r\n          console.warn(\'[drill:guided] stored resume set no longer matches this drill, starting fresh\', {\r\n            key: guidedSessionKey,\r\n          });\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Standalone Smart Drill and custom type drills are drawn\r\n      // fresh on every launch, so the stored queue IS the run: adopt it whole\r\n      // rather than comparing it to a new shuffle, or the student loses the\r\n      // questions they already answered. An explicit ?initialIndex wins, since\r\n  // controls.\r\n  const actionLockRef = React.useRef(false);\r\n  const [actionInFlight, setActionInFlight] = React.useState(false);\r\n  // Single-flight per question inside `handleSubmitNonAdaptive`: a second\r\n  // caller for the same qid receives the promise already in flight.\r\n  const pendingCommitsRef = React.useRef<Map<string, Promise<DrillSession[\'attempts\'] | null>>>(new Map());\r\n  // Official-layout-only local UI state (Find Text + Aa font scale).\r\n  const [officialSearchTerm, setOfficialSearchTerm] = React.useState(\'\');\r\n  const [officialFontScale, setOfficialFontScale] = React.useState(1);\r\n  // Smart Drill docked notes panel open state.\r\n  const [notesOpen, setNotesOpen] = React.useState(false);\r\n  // Active tab inside the docked drill drawer (Notes | Details).\r\n M docs/specs/2026-09-28-competitor-audit-coverage.md\n M src/lib/drillRunLedger.test.ts\n M src/lib/drillRunLedger.ts\n M src/lib/standaloneDrillHistory.ts\n M src/pages/Drill.tsx\n M src/pages/DrillRuns.test.tsx\n M src/pages/DrillRuns.tsx\n M src/pages/GuidedDrillStart.tsx\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '37:export interface DrillSession {\n     * Every choice the student put down on this question, in order, each\r\n     * stamped with how far into the question it was made. A single answer is\r\n     * a trail of one; changing A to B a minute in is two entries, and that is\r\n     * what the results screen draws as a timeline. Times accumulate across\r\n     * revisits, so a question returned to twice reads as one continuous run.\r\n     */\r\n    answerTrail?: Array<{ answer: string; atMs: number }>;\r\n    /**\r\n     * How many times the student opened this question, and how many of those\r\n     * visits ended with the answer exactly as they found it.\r\n     *\r\n     * A second pass that changes nothing is the webinar\'s most concrete\r\n     * finding — six questions revisited and left alone, because the student\r\n     * went back with the same reading that produced the wrong answer the first\r\n     * time. The answer trail records changes of mind; only this records a\r\n     * change of mind that never came.\r\n     */\r\n    visitCount?: number;\r\n    noChangeRevisits?: number;\r\n    /**\r\n     * Answer choices the student crossed off, in the order they crossed them.\r\n     *\r\n     * The runner has always tracked these and thrown them away at the end of\r\n     * the question. Kept, they answer the webinar\'s primary flagging question:\r\n     * "were you down to two?" Retry Finish.');\r\n      throw err;\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  /** Save guided work before moving, then reconcile its stable analytics rows. */\r\n  const persistOwnedDrillSession = async (source: DrillSession): Promise<void> => {\r\n    if (!guidedSessionKey) return persistStandaloneSession(source);\r\n    await persistGuidedSession(source);\r\n    const runId = guidedRunIdRef.current;\r\n    if (!runId) throw new Error('This guided drill has no saved sitting.');\r\n    for (const [qid, attempt] of source.attempts) {\r\n      const question = questionBank.getQuestion(qid);\r\n      if (!question || !attempt.selectedAnswer) continue;\r\n      const acknowledged = await saveAttemptToDatabase({\r\n        id: await guidedAttemptId(runId, qid, attempt.selectedAnswer),\r\n        qid,\r\n        correct: attempt.correct,\r\n        time_ms: attempt.timeMs,\r\n        qtype: question.qtype,\r\n        level: question.difficulty,\r\n        confidence: attempt.confidence ?? null,\r\n        mode: source.mode,\r\n        selected_answer: attempt.selectedAnswer,\r\n      });\r\n      if (!acknowledged) throw new Error('Could not save a guided drill answer to your account.');\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const finalizeGuidedAssignment = async (): Promise<void> => {\r\n    if (!guidedAssignmentId || !user) return;\r\n    try {\r\n      const { error } = await supabase\r\n        .from('guided_drill_assignments')\r\n        .update({ completed_at: new Date().toISOString(), status: 'completed' })\r\n        .eq('id', guidedAssignmentId)\r\n        .eq('student_id', user.id);\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n\r\n  // UI-level helper for the per-question submit handlers. Wraps the throwing\r\n  // service so the callers' `if (!saved.ok)` toast logic keeps working — the\r\n  // error is logged at the service AND visible to the user via the caller's\r\n  // toast. This does NOT silently swallow: failures are logged, classified\r\n  // where the error still exists, and surfaced through the typed result.\r\n  type PersistResult =\r\n    | { ok: true }\r\n    | { ok: false; errorKind: 'auth' | 'network' | 'db' | 'unknown' };\r\n  const classifyPersistError = (e: unknown): 'auth' | 'network' | 'db' | 'unknown' => {\r\n    const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e ?? '');\r\n    if (/no class_id and no user\\.id|jwt|auth|401|403/i.test(message)) return 'auth';\r\n    if (/fetch|network|timeout|Failed to fetch/i.test(message)) return 'network';\r\n    if (/insert failed|supabase|postgres|duplicate|violates/i.test(message)) return 'db';\r\n    return 'unknown';\r\n  };\r\n  // Structured failure event. No PII: no user id, no answer text. Returns a discriminated\r\n    // result on every path: `ok` with the attempts to carry forward (persisted,\r\n    // or nothing to persist), or `ok: false` when the save failed.\r\n    const commitPendingSelection = async (): Promise<\r\n      { ok: true; attempts: DrillSession[\'attempts\'] | null } | { ok: false }\r\n    > => {\r\n      if (isAdaptiveMode && selectedAnswer && !currentAttempted && !currentUnscored) {\r\n        toast.error(\'Submit this answer before ending Smart Drill.\');\r\n        return { ok: false };\r\n      }\r\n      if (isAdaptiveMode || !selectedAnswer || currentAttempted || currentUnscored) {\r\n        return { ok: true, attempts: null };\r\n      }\r\n      const attempts = await handleSubmitNonAdaptive(selectedAnswer);\r\n      if (!attempts) {\r\n            inlineNotes={explanationOpen ? inlineNotes : null}\r\n            highlights={questionHighlights.highlightsFor(currentQuestion.qid)}\r\n            highlightMode={highlightMode}\r\n            searchTerm={officialSearchTerm}\r\n            fontScale={officialFontScale}\r\n            onAnswerSelect={handleAnswerSelect}\r\n            onEliminate={handleEliminateAnswer}\r\n            onToggleFlag={handleToggleFlag}\r\n            onTextSelection={handleTextSelection}\r\n            onHighlightClick={handleHighlightClick}\r\n            // Review controls: Choice Notes as closed expanders, choices that\r\n            // stay live after a reveal, Reset Response, and the Argument\r\n            // Breakdown under the stimulus. All opt-in; the legacy modes get\r\n            // the pane exactly as before.\r\n            choiceNoteExpanders={reviewControlsAvailable}\r\n            choicesStayLive={reviewControlsAvailable}\r\n            interactionLocked={actionInFlight}\r\n            onResetResponse={reviewControlsAvailable ? handleResetResponse : undefined}\r\n            belowStimulus={\r\n              reviewControlsAvailable && explanationOpen ? (\r\n            // A real timed section is not a submit-one-at-a-time drill: the\r\n            // answer commits the moment it is selected, so Skip and Submit have\r\n            // nothing to do. What the section needs is the official navigator —\r\n            // the oval strip for the whole queue and prev/next arrows in the\r\n            // bottom-right corner, exactly where the paper-and-LawHub interface\r\n            // puts them. Leaving the section runs through Complete Section in\r\n            // the header strip.\r\n            <OfficialAnswerNavigator\r\n              questionQueue={session.questionQueue}\r\n              currentIndex={session.currentIndex}\r\n              attemptedQids={attemptedQids}\r\n              jumpEnabled={jumpEnabled}\r\n              jumpDisabledReason={timer?.isPaused ? \'Paused — resume to navigate\' : undefined}\r\n              showQuestionList\r\n              prevDisabled={session.currentIndex <= 0 || !!timer?.isPaused}\r\n              nextDisabled={\r\n                officialNextDisabled ||\r\n                session.currentIndex >= session.questionQueue.length - 1\r\n              }\r\n              nextDisabledReason={\r\n                timer?.isPaused\r\n                  ? \'Paused — resume to navigate\'\r\n                  : session.currentIndex >= session.questionQueue.length - 1\r\n                  ? \'Last question — use Complete Section to finish\'\r\n                  : undefined\r\n              }\r\n              onJumpTo={handleJumpTo}\r\n              onPrev={officialPrev}\r\n              onNext={() => { void officialNext(); }}\r\n              revealEnabled={revealSealed ? false : sectionRevealEnabled}\r\n              onRevealEnabledChange={revealSealed ? undefined : setSectionRevealEnabled}\r\n            />\r\n          ) : (\r\n            // Same chrome as the adaptive Smart Drill, but driven by the fixed\r\n            // queue the student hand-picked from the Smart LR Drill card. Submit\r\n            // saves and reveals in place; Next\r\n            // walks the queue via the shared officialNext; Skip advances past the\r\n            // answer gate; End finishes the section.\r\n            <OfficialSmartDrillBar\r\n              primaryAction={currentAttempted || currentUnscored ? \'next\' : \'submit\'}\r\n              primaryDisabled={!selectedAnswer || !!timer?.isPaused || actionInFlight}\r\n              onSubmit={() => {\r\n                void (async () => {\r\n                  const submitted = selectedAnswer;\r\n                  await handleSubmitNonAdaptive(submitted);\r\n                  if (autoReveal) {\r\n                    setShowAnswerRevealed(prev => new Map(prev).set(currentQuestion.qid, true));\r\n                  }\r\n                  // Pop the AI tutor (post-answer when revealed) on a wrong answer\r\n                  // so the student gets a specific, back-and-forth explanation\r\n                  // instead of the old dead "ANALYSIS VALID" placeholder.\r\n\r\n                {/* Submit button for non-adaptive, non-section, non-practice-set modes */}\r\n                {session.mode !== \'adaptive\' && session.mode !== \'full-section\' && !isPracticeSetMode && selectedAnswer && !showSolution && (\r\n                  <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-6 mt-2">\r\n                    <Button\r\n                      onClick={() => handleSubmitNonAdaptive()}\r\n                      size="lg"\r\n                      disabled={timer?.isPaused}\r\n                    >\r\n                      Check Answer\r\n                    </Button>\r\n                  </div>\r\n                )}\r\n\r\n                {/* Solution */}\r\n                {showSolution && (\r\n                  <div className="mt-6 p-6 border-t space-y-4">\r\n                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\r\n                      {selectedAnswer === currentQuestion.correctAnswer ? (\r\n                        <CheckCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-green-500" />\r\n                      ) : (\r\n                        <XCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-destructive" />\r\n                      )}\r\n                      <span className="font-semibold text-lg">\r\n                        {selectedAnswer === currentQuestion.correctAnswer\r\n                          ? \'Correct!\'\r\n                          : `The correct answer is (${currentQuestion.correctAnswer}).`}\r\n                      </span>\r\n'}]

Promise.resolve();\\r\\n  const current = previous.catch(() => undefined).then(write);\\r\\n  runWriteChains.set(runId, current);\\r\\n  void current.finally(() => {\\r\\n    if (runWriteChains.get(runId) === current) runWriteChains.delete(runId);\\r\\n  }).catch(() => undefined);\\r\\n  return current;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function assertOwner(userId: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\\r\\n  if (error || !data.user || data.user.id !== userId) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'Sign in to save or view your drills.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction parseOwned(userId: string, recordId: string, raw: unknown): StandaloneDrillRun | null {\\r\\n  const parsed = StandaloneDrillRunSchema.safeParse(raw);\\r\\n  if (!parsed.success) return null;\\r\\n  const row = parsed.data;\\r\\n  if (row.id !== recordId || row.user_id !== userId || row.session.id !== row.id || row.session.user_id !== userId) return null;\\r\\n  return { ...row, session: migratePracticeSession(row.session) };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction sessionFromSnapshot(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  runId: string,\\r\\n  sourceKey: string,\\r\\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n  createdAt: string,\\r\\n  updatedAt: string,\\r\\n  status: \'in_progress\' | \'completed\',\\r\\n  previous?: PracticeSession,\\r\\n): PracticeSession {\\r\\n  if (!snapshot.questionQueue.length && !previous?.question_queue.length) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'A drill needs questions before its run can be saved.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const queue = snapshot.questionQueue.length ? snapshot.questionQueue : previous!.question_queue;\\r\\n  return PracticeSession.parse({\\r\\n    id: runId,\\r\\n    user_id: userId,\\r\\n    pool_slug: sourceKey,\\r\\n    completion_id: sourceKey,\\r\\n    data_version: PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\\r\\n    question_queue: queue,\\r\\n    current_index: Math.min(Math.max(snapshot.currentIndex, 0), queue.length - 1),\\r\\n    attempts: snapshot.attempts,\\r\\n    status,\\r\\n    elapsed_ms: snapshot.elapsedMs ?? previous?.elapsed_ms,\\r\\n    timer_mode: snapshot.timerMode ?? previous?.timer_mode,\\r\\n    timer_custom_minutes: snapshot.timerCustomMinutes ?? previous?.timer_custom_minutes,\\r\\n    regulation_answered_qids: snapshot.regulationAnsweredQids ?? previous?.regulation_answered_qids,\\r\\n    started_at: createdAt,\\r\\n    updated_at: updatedAt,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function writeRun(row: StandaloneDrillRun): Promise<StandaloneDrillRun> {\\r\\n  const { error } = await supabase.from(\'bootcamp_kv\').upsert({\\r\\n    user_id: row.user_id,\\r\\n    table_name: TABLES.drill_runs,\\r\\n    record_id: row.id,\\r\\n    data: row,\\r\\n  }, { onConflict: \'user_id,table_name,record_id\' });\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  return row;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Create a new sitting only after its first snapshot is acknowledged by the account. */\\r\\nexport async function createStandaloneDrillRun(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  sourceKey: string,\\r\\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n  meta: StandaloneDrillRunMeta = {},\\r\\n): Promise<StandaloneDrillRun> {\\r\\n  await assertOwner(userId);\\r\\n  if (!(sourceKey.startsWith(\'drill::\') || sourceKey.startsWith(\'guided::\')) || [\\r\\n    \'drill::section::\',\\r\\n    \'drill::practice-test::\',\\r\\n    \'drill::assignment::\',\\r\\n    \'drill::bootcamp-day::\',\\r\\n  ].some((prefix) => sourceKey.startsWith(prefix))) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'A standalone drill needs a valid source key.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const id = crypto.randomUUID();\\r\\n  const time = new Date().toISOString();\\r\\n  const row = StandaloneDrillRunSchema.parse({\\r\\n    id,\\r\\n    user_id: userId,\\r\\n    source_key: sourceKey,\\r\\n    mode: meta.mode ?? \'\',\\r\\n    label: meta.label ?? \'\',\\r\\n    session: sessionFromSnapshot(userId, id, sourceKey, snapshot, time, time, \'in_progress\'),\\r\\n    created_at: time,\\r\\n    updated_at: time,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  return withRun(id, () => writeRun(row));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** A missing, malformed, or different owner\'s row is never returned. */\\r\\nexport async function loadStandaloneDrillRun(userId: string, runId: string): Promise<StandaloneDrillRun | null> {\\r\\n  await assertOwner(userId);\\r\\n  if (!z.string().uuid().safeParse(runId).success) return null;\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n    .select(\'record_id,data\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'table_name\', TABLES.drill_runs)\\r\\n    .eq(\'record_id\', runId)\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  return data ? parseOwned(userId, data.record_id, data.data) : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Page through account rows so older sittings do not disappear at the API limit. */\\r\\nexport async function listStandaloneDrillRuns(userId: string): Promise<StandaloneDrillRun[]> {\\r\\n  await assertOwner(userId);\\r\\n  const runs: StandaloneDrillRun[] = [];\\r\\n  const pageSize = 500;\\r\\n  for (let offset = 0; ; offset += pageSize) {\\r\\n    const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n      .select(\'record_id,data\')\\r\\n      .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n      .eq(\'table_name\', TABLES.drill_runs)\\r\\n      .order(\'record_id\', { ascending: true })\\r\\n      .range(offset, offset + pageSize - 1);\\r\\n    if (error) throw error;\\r\\n    for (const item of data ?? []) {\\r\\n      const run = parseOwned(userId, item.record_id, item.data);\\r\\n      if (run) runs.push(run);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if ((data ?? []).length < pageSize) break;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return runs.sort((a, b) => b.updated_at.localeCompare(a.updated_at));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function updateRun(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  runId: string,\\r\\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n  status: \'in_progress\' | \'completed\',\\r\\n): Promise<StandaloneDrillRun> {\\r\\n  return withRun(runId, async () => {\\r\\n    const current = await loadStandaloneDrillRun(userId, runId);\\r\\n    if (!current) throw new Error(\'This drill run could not be found in your account.\');\\r\\n    if (current.session.status === \'completed\') return current;\\r\\n    const time = new Date().toISOString();\\r\\n    const row = StandaloneDrillRunSchema.parse({\\r\\n      ...current,\\r\\n      session: sessionFromSnapshot(\\r\\n        userId, runId, current.source_key, snapshot, current.created_at, time, status, current.session,\\r\\n      ),\\r\\n      updated_at: time,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    return writeRun(row);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function saveStandaloneDrillRun(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  runId: string,\\r\\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n): Promise<StandaloneDrillRun> {\\r\\n  return updateRun(userId, runId, snapshot, \'in_progress\');\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function completeStandaloneDrillRun(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  runId: string,\\r\\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n): Promise<StandaloneDrillRun> {\\r\\n  return updateRun(userId, runId, snapshot, \'completed\');\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

Unlimited has\r\n * no budget, which is not the same as a budget of zero.\r\n */\r\nfunction sectionBudgetMs(timer?: TimerConfig): number | undefined {\r\n  const sectionTimerConfig = session?.fullSectionConfig?.timer;\r\n  const sectionElapsedMs = React.useCallback((): number | undefined => {\r\n    const clock = timerRef.current;\r\n    if (!clock) return undefined;\r\n    if (clock.mode === 'stopwatch') return Math.max(0, Math.round(clock.ms));\r\n    const total = sectionBudgetMs(sectionTimerConfig);\r\n    if (!total) return undefined;\r\n    return Math.max(0, Math.round(total - clock.ms));\r\n  }, [sectionTimerConfig]);\r\n\r\n  const drillResumeSnapshot = React.useCallback(\r\n    (source: DrillSession | null): PracticeSessionSnapshot | null => {\r\n      if (!source) return null;\r\n      return {\r\n        questionQueue: source.questionQueue,\r\n        currentIndex: source.currentIndex,\r\n        attempts: Array.from(source.attempts.entries())\r\n          .filter(([, attempt]) => !!attempt.selectedAnswer)\r\n          .map(([qid, attempt]) => ({\r\n            qid,\r\n            selected_answer: attempt.selectedAnswer,\r\n            correct: !!attempt.correct,\r\n            time_ms: Number.isFinite(attempt.timeMs) ? Math.max(0, Math.round(attempt.timeMs)) : 0,\r\n            switch_count: attempt.switchCount ?? 0,\r\n            flagged: !!attempt.brMarked,\r\n            answer_revealed: !!attempt.answerRevealed,\r\n            answer_trail: attempt.answerTrail?.map((e) => ({\r\n              answer: e.answer,\r\n              at_ms: Math.max(0, Math.round(e.atMs)),\r\n            })),\r\n          })),\r\n        // Stored as time SPENT, so it reads the same whether the surface\r\n        // counts down or up.\r\n        elapsedMs: sectionElapsedMs(),\r\n        timerMode: source.fullSectionConfig?.timer.mode,\r\n        timerCustomMinutes: source.fullSectionConfig?.timer.customMinutes,\r\n        regulationAnsweredQids: source.regulationMark?.answeredQids,\r\n      };\r\n    },\r\n    [sectionElapsedMs],\r\n  );\r\n    }\r\n    let saved = 0;\r\n    let wajFailed = 0;\r\n    let timeMsZero = 0;\r\n    const attemptErrors: unknown[] = [];\r\n    const savedQids: string[] = [];\r\n    const wajQids: string[] = [];\r\n\r\n    for (const [qid, attempt] of attempts) {\r\n      const question = questionBank.getQuestion(qid);\r\n      if (!question || !attempt.selectedAnswer) continue;\r\n\r\n      if (!Number.isFinite(attempt.timeMs) || attempt.timeMs <= 0) {\r\n        timeMsZero++;\r\n        console.warn('[commitSessionAttempts] non-positive timeMs', {\r\n          qid,\r\n          timeMs: attempt.timeMs,\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      try {\r\n        const ok = await saveAttemptToDatabase({\r\n          id: guidedSessionKey && guidedRunIdRef.current\r\n            ? await guidedAttemptId(guidedRunIdRef.current, qid)\r\n            : undefined,\r\n          qid,\r\n          correct: attempt.correct,\r\n          time_ms: attempt.timeMs,\r\n          qtype: question.qtype,\r\n          level: question.difficulty,\r\n          confidence: attempt.confidence ?? null,\r\n          mode,\r\n          selected_answer: attempt.selectedAnswer,\r\n        });\r\n        if (ok === false) {\r\n          // Cold-start skip: classId not yet resolved. Treat as failure so the\r\n"}]

data: [...account.rows.entries()]\r\n            .filter(([id]) => !filters.record_id || id === filters.record_id)\r\n            .map(([record_id, data]) => ({ record_id, data }))\r\n },\r\n}));\r\n\r\nimport {\r\n  completeStandaloneDrillRun,\r\n  createStandaloneDrillRun,\r\n  listStandaloneDrillRuns,\r\n  loadStandaloneDrillRun,\r\n  saveStandaloneDrillRun,\r\n} from [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': " * Only the fields the results view uses are populated. `timestamp` is not\r\n * stored per attempt (the snapshot schema keeps qid/answer/correct/time_ms), so\r\n * it is left at 0 rather than invented — the results view sorts by queue order\r\n * and never reads it.\r\n */\r\nexport function guidedSessionToDrillSession(\r\n  stored: GuidedDrillSession | null,\r\n): Pick<DrillSession, 'mode' | 'questionQueue' | 'currentIndex' | 'attempts'> | null {\r\n  if (!stored || stored.question_queue.length === 0) return null;\r\n  const attempts: DrillSession['attempts'] = new Map();\r\n  for (const a of stored.attempts) {\r\n    attempts.set(a.qid, {\r\n      selectedAnswer: a.selected_answer,\r\n      correct: a.correct,\r\n      timeMs: a.time_ms ?? 0,\r\n      timestamp: 0,\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n  return {\r\n    mode: 'type-drill',\r\n    questionQueue: stored.question_queue,\r\n    currentIndex: stored.current_index,\r\n    attempts,\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The finished run for this drill, if there is one. Mirrors\r\n * loadGuidedDrillSession but selects the completed row instead of the resumable\r\n * one, so the launch page can offer a review rather than silently redrawing.\r\n */\r\nexport async function loadCompletedGuidedDrillSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  legacyKey?: string | null,\r\n): Promise<GuidedDrillSession | null> {\r\n  const stored = await loadGuidedDrillSession(userId, key, legacyKey);\r\n  if (!stored || stored.status !== 'completed') return null;\r\n  return stored.attempts.length > 0 ? stored : null;\r\n}\r\n  AlertDialogHeader,\r\n  AlertDialogTitle,\r\n} from '@/components/ui/alert-dialog';\r\nimport type { LRQuestion } from '@/lib/questionLoader';\r\nimport type {\r\n  BlindReviewResult as DrillBlindReviewResult,\r\n  DrillMode,\r\n  DrillSession,\r\n  FullSectionConfig,\r\n  TimerConfig,\r\n  TypeDrillConfig,\r\n} from '@/types/drill';\r\nimport { QuestionPoolService, servesFixedQueue, type QuestionUsage } from '@/lib/questionPoolService';\r\nimport { QuestionPoolChip } from '@/components/drill/QuestionPoolChip';\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '/**\r\n * Resume state for guided drills.\r\n *\r\n * A student who starts an assigned guided drill and leaves — taps back, closes\r\n * the tab, comes back after dinner — must land on the question they stopped on,\r\n * with the SAME questions and every answer they already gave still marked.\r\n * Neither React state nor URL params can do that: both die with the page, and\r\n * re-entering from the Classroom re-runs the level draw, which reshuffles the\r\n * pool and hands the student a different set of questions. That is the bug this\r\n * module exists to close.\r\n *\r\n * It is a thin adapter, not a second mechanism. The Intro to LR practice sets\r\n * already solved exactly this problem (see practiceSessionStore), and that store\r\n * gives us local-first writes, a debounced `bootcamp_kv` cloud upsert, zod\r\n * validation, and cross-device hydration. Guided drills ride the same rows,\r\n * namespaced by key so the two can never collide:\r\n *\r\n *     practice set   →  pool_slug "mc-practice-1"\r\n *     assigned drill →  pool_slug "guided::<assignmentId>"\r\n *     direct drill   →  pool_slug "guided::drill:<drillId>"\r\n *\r\n * The `pool_slug`/`completion_id` field names come from that store\'s schema and\r\n * are reused as-is rather than forked; read them as "which drill" and "what it\r\n * completes", not as bootcamp-only concepts.\r\n *\r\n * Failure policy, inherited: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must\r\n * never block starting a drill), writes surface to the caller.\r\n */\r\n'}]
